--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 1705
entity_slug: the-sloop
google_review_name: places/ChIJocYu8wYFmogRWwAiSn2fhRg/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT201bVgwWnZNVEZVTW1kT2MzbDROa0l3TkZoQlExRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐

If you are anywhere near Gulf Shores and you love a good Philly cheesesteak, you absolutely have to stop by The Sloop. I cannot say enough about how good this sandwich was. I went with the cheese whiz because that’s how the real ones in Philly are traditionally made and I am so glad I did. The flavor combination was perfect. The meat was juicy and tender, the cheese was creamy and melted perfectly through every bite, and the grilled onions, mushrooms, and peppers brought the whole thing together in the best way possible.

It also comes with homemade potato chips that are just next level. You can tell they were made fresh and they have that perfect balance of crunch and salt that makes you want to keep eating even after you are full. I also tried the Philly eggrolls and those were a total hit with my wife. Crispy on the outside, loaded with the same savory Philly cheesesteak filling inside, they nailed it.

The atmosphere is exactly what you want in a local Gulf Shores spot. It has that cool laid back dive bar vibe where everyone feels welcome. They have live music out back most nights and the place sits right under the bridge which makes it a fun and easy stop whether you are coming off the water or just exploring town.

As for the value, the pricing is awesome. Ten bucks for the 5 1/4 inch sandwich or eighteen bucks for the big 11 1/2 inch version and trust me every penny is worth it. I went for the full size and did not regret it for a second. It is one of those meals that just hits all the right notes. Hearty, flavorful, and made with care.

If you are in Gulf Shores, do yourself a favor and grab a Philly at The Sloop. You will not be disappointed.

review_text: My family and I visited on Fall Break and we loved this little hole in the wall. We were looking for something a little different than the tourist traps off the beach and this place did not disappoint. The waitress was sweet and did a great job. She was very impressive as she did not even have to write anything down (there was 6 of us) and the entire order came out correctly! Everything came out quickly and without a single issue. We had two appetizers, the cheesesteak egg rolls and the stuffed mushrooms. They were our favorites! Great place! Amazing food! Amazing staff! The prices were great to. It was about $70 for 4 of us. Not bad at all.

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: 7077dafd-d231-4869-b6f7-bec5853fc9fc
entity_slug: the-sloop
reviewer_name: The Chef’s View
rating: 5
body: ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐

If you are anywhere near Gulf Shores and you love a good Philly cheesesteak, you absolutely have to stop by The Sloop. I cannot say enough about how good this sandwich was. I went with the cheese whiz because that’s how the real ones in Philly are traditionally made and I am so glad I did. The flavor combination was perfect. The meat was juicy and tender, the cheese was creamy and melted perfectly through every bite, and the grilled onions, mushrooms, and peppers brought the whole thing together in the best way possible.

It also comes with homemade potato chips that are just next level. You can tell they were made fresh and they have that perfect balance of crunch and salt that makes you want to keep eating even after you are full. I also tried the Philly eggrolls and those were a total hit with my wife. Crispy on the outside, loaded with the same savory Philly cheesesteak filling inside, they nailed it.

The atmosphere is exactly what you want in a local Gulf Shores spot. It has that cool laid back dive bar vibe where everyone feels welcome. They have live music out back most nights and the place sits right under the bridge which makes it a fun and easy stop whether you are coming off the water or just exploring town.

As for the value, the pricing is awesome. Ten bucks for the 5 1/4 inch sandwich or eighteen bucks for the big 11 1/2 inch version and trust me every penny is worth it. I went for the full size and did not regret it for a second. It is one of those meals that just hits all the right notes. Hearty, flavorful, and made with care.

If you are in Gulf Shores, do yourself a favor and grab a Philly at The Sloop. You will not be disappointed.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true
created_at: 2025-10-21T22:04:49.453172+00:00
updated_at: 2026-06-30T13:52:51.804126+00:00
